[17 CFR 240.14c-2](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.14c-2): Official (a)(1) “In connection with every annual or other meeting of the holders of the class of securities registered pursuant to section 12 of the Act or of a class of securities issued by an investment company registered under the Investment Company Act of 1940 that has made a public offering of securities, including the taking of corporate action by the written authorization or consent of security holders, the registrant shall transmit to every security holder of the class that is entitled to vote or give an authorization or consent in regard to any matter to be acted upon and from whom proxy authorization or consent is not solicited on behalf of the registrant pursuant to section 14(a) of the Act:” Official (a)(1)(i) “A written information statement containing the information specified in Schedule 14C (§ 240.14c-101);” Official (a)(1)(ii) “A publicly-filed information statement, in the form and manner described in § 240.14c-3(d), containing the information specified in Schedule 14C (§ 240.14c-101); or” Official (a)(1)(iii) “A written information statement included in a registration statement filed under the Securities Act of 1933 on Form S-4 or F-4 (§ 239.25 or § 239.34 of this chapter) or Form N-14 (§ 239.23 of this chapter) and containing the information specified in such Form.” Official (a)(2) “Notwithstanding paragraph (a)(1) of this section:” Official (a)(2)(i) “In the case of a class of securities in unregistered or bearer form, such statements need to be transmitted only to those security holders whose names are known to the registrant; and” Official (a)(2)(ii) “No such statements need to be transmitted to a security holder if a registrant would be excused from delivery of an annual report to security holders or a proxy statement under § 240.14a-3(e)(2) if such section were applicable.” Official (b) “The information statement shall be sent or given at least 20 calendar days prior to the meeting date or, in the case of corporate action taken pursuant to the consents or authorizations of security holders, at least 20 calendar days prior to the earliest date on which the corporate action may be taken.” Official (c) “If a transaction is a roll-up transaction as defined in Item 901(c) of Regulation S-K (17 CFR 229.901(c)) and is registered (or authorized to be registered) on Form S-4 (17 CFR 229.25) or Form F-4 (17 CFR 229.34), the information statement must be distributed to security holders no later than the lesser of 60 calendar days prior to the date on which the meeting of security holders is held or action is taken, or the maximum number of days permitted for giving notice under applicable state law.” Official (d) “A registrant shall transmit an information statement to security holders pursuant to paragraph (a) of this section by satisfying the requirements set forth in § 240.14a-16; provided, however, that the registrant shall revise the information required in the Notice of Internet Availability of Proxy Materials, including changing the title of that notice, to reflect the fact that the registrant is not soliciting proxies for the meeting.” Official (e) “If a transaction is a de-SPAC transaction, as defined in § 229.1601(a) of this chapter (Item 1601(a) of Regulation S-K), the information statement of the special purpose acquisition company, as defined in § 229.1601(b) (Item 1601(b) of Regulation S-K), must be distributed to security holders no later than the lesser of 20 calendar days prior to the date on which the meeting of security holders is to be held or action is to be taken in connection with the de-SPAC transaction or the maximum number of days permitted for disseminating the information statement under the applicable laws of the jurisdiction of incorporation or organization.” Official caption typography is “Distribution of information statement.” Official source line: “[51 FR 42070, Nov. 20, 1986, as amended at 56 FR 57254, Nov. 8, 1991; **Lab:** section BR of the same `16_index` script (after section BQ / 14c-4; 275/275 after BR):

```text
=== BR. 17 CFR 240.14c-2: Distribution of information statement ===
official caption typography is Distribution of information statement.
14c-2(a)(1): In connection with every annual or other meeting of the holders of the class of securities registered pursuant to section 12 of the Act or of a class of securities issued by an investment company registered under the Investment Company Act of 1940 that has made a public offering of securities, including the taking of corporate action by the written authorization or consent of security holders, the registrant shall transmit to every security holder of the class that is entitled to vote or give an authorization or consent in regard to any matter to be acted upon and from whom proxy authorization or consent is not solicited on behalf of the registrant pursuant to section 14(a) of the Act:
14c-2(a)(1)(i): A written information statement containing the information specified in Schedule 14C (§ 240.14c-101);
14c-2(a)(1)(ii): A publicly-filed information statement, in the form and manner described in § 240.14c-3(d), containing the information specified in Schedule 14C (§ 240.14c-101); or
14c-2(a)(1)(iii): A written information statement included in a registration statement filed under the Securities Act of 1933 on Form S-4 or F-4 (§ 239.25 or § 239.34 of this chapter) or Form N-14 (§ 239.23 of this chapter) and containing the information specified in such Form.
14c-2(a)(2): Notwithstanding paragraph (a)(1) of this section:
14c-2(a)(2)(i): In the case of a class of securities in unregistered or bearer form, such statements need to be transmitted only to those security holders whose names are known to the registrant; and
14c-2(a)(2)(ii): No such statements need to be transmitted to a security holder if a registrant would be excused from delivery of an annual report to security holders or a proxy statement under § 240.14a-3(e)(2) if such section were applicable.
14c-2(b): The information statement shall be sent or given at least 20 calendar days prior to the meeting date or, in the case of corporate action taken pursuant to the consents or authorizations of security holders, at least 20 calendar days prior to the earliest date on which the corporate action may be taken.
14c-2(c): If a transaction is a roll-up transaction as defined in Item 901(c) of Regulation S-K (17 CFR 229.901(c)) and is registered (or authorized to be registered) on Form S-4 (17 CFR 229.25) or Form F-4 (17 CFR 229.34), the information statement must be distributed to security holders no later than the lesser of 60 calendar days prior to the date on which the meeting of security holders is held or action is taken, or the maximum number of days permitted for giving notice under applicable state law.
14c-2(d): A registrant shall transmit an information statement to security holders pursuant to paragraph (a) of this section by satisfying the requirements set forth in § 240.14a-16; provided, however, that the registrant shall revise the information required in the Notice of Internet Availability of Proxy Materials, including changing the title of that notice, to reflect the fact that the registrant is not soliciting proxies for the meeting.
14c-2(e): If a transaction is a de-SPAC transaction, as defined in § 229.1601(a) of this chapter (Item 1601(a) of Regulation S-K), the information statement of the special purpose acquisition company, as defined in § 229.1601(b) (Item 1601(b) of Regulation S-K), must be distributed to security holders no later than the lesser of 20 calendar days prior to the date on which the meeting of security holders is to be held or action is to be taken in connection with the de-SPAC transaction or the maximum number of days permitted for disseminating the information statement under the applicable laws of the jurisdiction of incorporation or organization.

## 17 CFR 240.14a-21: Shareholder approval of executive compensation, frequency of votes for approval of executive compensation and shareholder approval of golden parachute compensation (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 14a-5 / already-taught 14a-3 / 14a-9 / 14a-10 / 14a-14 stay as written. This box packs the whole current official 14a-21 section as the say-on-pay, frequency, and golden-parachute shareholder-advisory-vote core for US listed common stock. Official typography uses typographic U+201C/U+201D around the sample “RESOLVED, that the compensation paid to the company's named executive officers, as disclosed pursuant to Item 402 of Regulation S-K, including the Compensation Discussion and Analysis, compensation tables and narrative discussion is hereby APPROVED.” Official apostrophe in “company's” is ASCII U+0027; do not tidy it into U+2019. Official typography italicizes the letter l in official Instruction 3 Item 402(<em>l</em>) of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402(<em>l</em>) of this chapter). Official Instruction 4 typography is 15 U.S.C 77a (no period after C) then italic <em>et seq.</em> Official instruction headings use `<E T="01">a</E>` in “Instruction to paragraph (a):” and “Instructions to § 240.14a-21:” (official source typography; pack the official visible heading typography). Official CITA is “[76 FR 6045, Feb. 2, 2011, as amended at 82 FR 17555, Apr. 12, 2017]”. Official API XML has no SECAUTH; do not invent a source line. Official 12b-2 and official 14a-20 (TARP) are named only; do not pack 14a-20. Learning only; no issuer or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.14a-21](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.14a-21): Official (a) “If a solicitation is made by a registrant, other than an emerging growth company as defined in Rule 12b-2 (§ 240.12b-2), and the solicitation relates to an annual or other meeting of shareholders at which directors will be elected and for which the rules of the Commission require executive compensation disclosure pursuant to Item 402 of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402 of this chapter), the registrant shall, for the first annual or other meeting of shareholders on or after January 21, 2011, or for the first annual or other meeting of shareholders on or after January 21, 2013 if the registrant is a smaller reporting company, and thereafter no later than the annual or other meeting of shareholders held in the third calendar year after the immediately preceding vote under this subsection, include a separate resolution subject to shareholder advisory vote to approve the compensation of its named executive officers, as disclosed pursuant to Item 402 of Regulation S-K.” Official Instruction to paragraph (a) “The registrant's resolution shall indicate that the shareholder advisory vote under this subsection is to approve the compensation of the registrant's named executive officers as disclosed pursuant to Item 402 of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402 of this chapter). The following is a non-exclusive example of a resolution that would satisfy the requirements of this subsection: “RESOLVED, that the compensation paid to the company's named executive officers, as disclosed pursuant to Item 402 of Regulation S-K, including the Compensation Discussion and Analysis, compensation tables and narrative discussion is hereby APPROVED.”” Official (b) “If a solicitation is made by a registrant, other than an emerging growth company as defined in Rule 12b-2 (§ 240.12b-2), and the solicitation relates to an annual or other meeting of shareholders at which directors will be elected and for which the rules of the Commission require executive compensation disclosure pursuant to Item 402 of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402 of this chapter), the registrant shall, for the first annual or other meeting of shareholders on or after January 21, 2011, or for the first annual or other meeting of shareholders on or after January 21, 2013 if the registrant is a smaller reporting company, and thereafter no later than the annual or other meeting of shareholders held in the sixth calendar year after the immediately preceding vote under this subsection, include a separate resolution subject to shareholder advisory vote as to whether the shareholder vote required by paragraph (a) of this section should occur every 1, 2 or 3 years. Registrants required to provide a separate shareholder vote pursuant to § 240.14a-20 of this chapter shall include the separate resolution required by this section for the first annual or other meeting of shareholders after the registrant has repaid all obligations arising from financial assistance provided under the TARP, as defined in section 3(8) of the Emergency Economic Stabilization Act of 2008 (12 U.S.C. 5202(8)), and thereafter no later than the annual or other meeting of shareholders held in the sixth calendar year after the immediately preceding vote under this subsection.” Official (c) “If a solicitation is made by a registrant, other than an emerging growth company as defined in Rule 12b-2 (§ 240.12b-2), for a meeting of shareholders at which shareholders are asked to approve an acquisition, merger, consolidation or proposed sale or other disposition of all or substantially all the assets of the registrant, the registrant shall include a separate resolution subject to shareholder advisory vote to approve any agreements or understandings and compensation disclosed pursuant to Item 402(t) of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402(t) of this chapter), unless such agreements or understandings have been subject to a shareholder advisory vote under paragraph (a) of this section. Consistent with section 14A(b) of the Exchange Act (15 U.S.C. 78n-1(b)), any agreements or understandings between an acquiring company and the named executive officers of the registrant, where the registrant is not the acquiring company, are not required to be subject to the separate shareholder advisory vote under this paragraph.” Official Instruction 1 “Disclosure relating to the compensation of directors required by Item 402(k) (§ 229.402(k) of this chapter) and Item 402(r) of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402(r) of this chapter) is not subject to the shareholder vote required by paragraph (a) of this section. If a registrant includes disclosure pursuant to Item 402(s) of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402(s) of this chapter) about the registrant's compensation policies and practices as they relate to risk management and risk-taking incentives, these policies and practices would not be subject to the shareholder vote required by paragraph (a) of this section. To the extent that risk considerations are a material aspect of the registrant's compensation policies or decisions for named executive officers, the registrant is required to discuss them as part of its Compensation Discussion and Analysis under § 229.402(b) of this chapter, and therefore such disclosure would be considered by shareholders when voting on executive compensation.” Official Instruction 2 “If a registrant includes disclosure of golden parachute compensation arrangements pursuant to Item 402(t) (§ 229.402(t) of this chapter) in an annual meeting proxy statement, such disclosure would be subject to the shareholder advisory vote required by paragraph (a) of this section.” Official Instruction 3 “Registrants that are smaller reporting companies entitled to provide scaled disclosure in accordance with Item 402(<em>l</em>) of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402(<em>l</em>) of this chapter) are not required to include a Compensation Discussion and Analysis in their proxy statements in order to comply with this section. For smaller reporting companies, the vote required by paragraph (a) of this section must be to approve the compensation of the named executive officers as disclosed pursuant to Item 402(m) through (q) of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402(m) through (q) of this chapter).” Official Instruction 4 “A registrant that has ceased being an emerging growth company shall include the first separate resolution described under § 240.14a-21(a) not later than the end of (i) in the case of a registrant that was an emerging growth company for less than two years after the date of first sale of common equity securities of the registrant pursuant to an effective registration statement under the Securities Act of 1933 (15 U.S.C 77a <em>et seq.</em>), the three-year period beginning on such date; and (ii) in the case of any other registrant, the one-year period beginning on the date the registrant is no longer an emerging growth company.” Official caption typography is “Shareholder approval of executive compensation, frequency of votes for approval of executive compensation and shareholder approval of golden parachute compensation.” Official source line: “[76 FR 6045, Feb. 2, 2011, as amended at 82 FR 17555, Apr. 12, 2017]”. Official API XML has no SECAUTH; do not invent a source line. Official typography uses typographic U+201C/U+201D around the sample “RESOLVED, that the compensation paid to the company's named executive officers, as disclosed pursuant to Item 402 of Regulation S-K, including the Compensation Discussion and Analysis, compensation tables and narrative discussion is hereby APPROVED.” Official apostrophe in “company's” is ASCII U+0027. Official typography italicizes the letter l in official Instruction 3 Item 402(<em>l</em>) of Regulation S-K (§ 229.402(<em>l</em>) of this chapter). Official Instruction 4 typography is 15 U.S.C 77a (no period after C) then italic <em>et seq.</em> Official instruction headings use `<E T="01">a</E>` in “Instruction to paragraph (a):” and “Instructions to § 240.14a-21:” (official source typography; pack the official visible heading typography). Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17.

Official 12b-2 and official 14a-20 (TARP) are named only; do not pack 14a-20. Packed 14a-5 and already-taught 14a-3 / 14a-9 / 14a-10 / 14a-14 stay as written.

## 17 CFR 240.12b-20: additional material information in a statement or report (checked 2026-11-12)

Packed 14a-9 above, packed 10b-5, and already-taught Regulation FD and Form 8-K Item 5.07, stay as written. Do not rewrite those already-shipped boxes. This section adds official 12b-20 so a student does not flatten additional material information in a statement or report into packed 14a-9 or 10b-5, tidy the official typography into those rules' wording, or insert a comma after “made.” Learning only; no issuer or disclosure advice.

[17 CFR 240.12b-20](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-20): “In addition to the information expressly required to be included in a statement or report, there shall be added such further material information, if any, as may be necessary to make the required statements, in the light of the circumstances under which they are made not misleading.” Official caption is “Additional information.” Official typography is “in the light of the circumstances under which they are made not misleading” (no comma after “made”). Do not insert a comma after “made,” and do not tidy that clause into packed 10b-5’s “in the light of the circumstances under which they were made” or packed 14a-9’s “in the light of the circumstances under which it is made.” LII HTML may insert a conversion space before “not misleading”; that space is not official typography. Cite 17 CFR 240.12b-20. Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` is **2026-11-09**. Do not flatten 12b-20 into packed 14a-9, packed 10b-5, already-taught Regulation FD, Form 8-K Item 5.07, or 14a-8. Do not invent a scienter / materiality / judicial-opinion catalog, and do not invent a form-specific clock in this rule.

## 17 CFR 240.13a-14(a)/(c): Certification of disclosure in annual and quarterly reports (checked 2026-11-12)

Packed 12b-20 above, already-taught Form 10-K / 10-Q clocks, already-taught Form 20-F four-month / Form 6-K promptly clocks, and Form 8-K Item 4.02 stay as written. Do not rewrite those already-shipped boxes. This section packs official (a) and official (c) so a student does not treat those filing clocks as the certification-of-disclosure rule, or invent that a required certification may be signed under a power of attorney. Learning only; no issuer or disclosure advice.

[17 CFR 240.13a-14](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13a-14)(a): “Each report, including transition reports, filed on Form 10-Q, Form 10-K, Form 20-F or Form 40-F (§ 249.308a, § 249.310, § 249.220f or § 249.240f of this chapter) under Section 13(a) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78m(a)), other than a report filed by an Asset-Backed Issuer (as defined in § 229.1101 of this chapter) or a report on Form 20-F filed under § 240.13a-19, must include certifications in the form specified in the applicable exhibit filing requirements of such report and such certifications must be filed as an exhibit to such report. Each principal executive and principal financial officer of the issuer, or persons performing similar functions, at the time of filing of the report must sign a certification.” Official caption typography is “Certification of disclosure in annual and quarterly reports.” Official typography is “Form 10-Q, Form 10-K, Form 20-F or Form 40-F” (no Oxford comma). Official (c): “A person required to provide a certification specified in paragraph (a), (b) or (d) of this section may not have the certification signed on his or her behalf pursuant to a power of attorney or other form of confirming authority.” Cite 17 CFR 240.13a-14. Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` is **2026-11-09**. Official (b) requires that each periodic report containing financial statements filed pursuant to section 13(a) must be accompanied by the certifications required by 18 U.S.C. 1350 and that such certifications must be furnished as an exhibit; name official (b), do not pack the 18 U.S.C. 1350 furnished-certification catalog as the teaching core (official (a) certifications are *filed* as an exhibit; official (b) certifications are *furnished*). Official (d)/(e) asset-backed certification catalog, official (f) Interactive Data File exception, and the official (a) ICFR phase-in language that names § 240.13a-15 or § 240.15d-15 exist; name them, do not build those catalogs, and do not pack 13a-15. 17 CFR 240.15d-14 (same official caption; the Section 15(d) companion, naming § 240.15d-19 rather than § 240.13a-19) is named only; do not pack 15d-14 as a second teaching core. Do not flatten 13a-14 into already-taught Form 10-K / 10-Q clocks, packed 12b-20, already-taught Form 20-F four-month / Form 6-K promptly clocks, or Form 8-K Item 4.02. Do not invent a certification form number or a filing clock in this rule.

## 17 CFR 240.13b2-1: Falsification of accounting records (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 10b-5, packed 12b-20, and packed 13a-14 stay as written. This box is a different rule: “No person shall directly or indirectly, falsify or cause to be falsified, any book, record or account subject to section 13(b)(2)(A) of the Securities Exchange Act.” Keep the comma after “indirectly,” and the comma after “falsified.” Official typography is “book, record or account” (no Oxford comma), lowercase “section 13(b)(2)(A),” and “the Securities Exchange Act” (that sentence does not add “of 1934”). Official caption typography is “Falsification of accounting records.” Official SECAUTH typography is 15 U.S.C. 78m(b)(2); 15 U.S.C. 78m(a), 78m(b)(1), 78o(d), 78j(b), 78n(a), 78t(b), 78t(c). Official source line: “[44 FR 10970, Feb. 23, 1979].” Name 13b2-2 only; do not pack that catalog, and do not flatten this rule into packed 10b-5, packed 12b-20, or packed 13a-14. Learning only; no issuer, filer, or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.13b2-1](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13b2-1): “No person shall directly or indirectly, falsify or cause to be falsified, any book, record or account subject to section 13(b)(2)(A) of the Securities Exchange Act.” Official caption typography is “Falsification of accounting records.” Official SECAUTH typography is 15 U.S.C. 78m(b)(2); 15 U.S.C. 78m(a), 78m(b)(1), 78o(d), 78j(b), 78n(a), 78t(b), 78t(c). Official source line: “[44 FR 10970, Feb. 23, 1979].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17.

Official 13b2-2 is named only; this box does not pack that catalog. Packed 10b-5, packed 12b-20, and packed 13a-14 exist and are named only.

## 17 CFR 240.10D-1(b)(1): recover reasonably promptly the amount of erroneously awarded incentive-based compensation (checked 2026-11-12)

The new 13a-14 section above, packed 12b-20, already-taught Form 8-K Item 4.02, and already-taught Form 10-K / 10-Q clocks stay as written. Do not rewrite those already-shipped boxes. This section packs official (b)(1)’s opening written-policy sentence only, so a student does not invent a numeric clock for “reasonably promptly,” or flatten this listing-standard recovery policy into already-taught Item 4.02, packed 12b-20, packed 13a-14, or already-taught 10-K / 10-Q clocks. Learning only; no issuer, compensation, or disclosure advice.

[17 CFR 240.10D-1](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.10D-1)(b): “The issuer must:” Official (b)(1): “Adopt and comply with a written policy providing that the issuer will recover reasonably promptly the amount of erroneously awarded incentive-based compensation in the event that the issuer is required to prepare an accounting restatement due to the material noncompliance of the issuer with any financial reporting requirement under the securities laws, including any required accounting restatement to correct an error in previously issued financial statements that is material to the previously issued financial statements, or that would result in a material misstatement if the error were corrected in the current period or left uncorrected in the current period.” Official caption typography is “Listing standards relating to recovery of erroneously awarded compensation.” Official typography is “recover reasonably promptly.” Cite 17 CFR 240.10D-1. Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` is **2026-11-09**. Official (a) is listing-standard machinery directed at national securities exchanges and associations, including historical 2023 transition dates; name official (a), do not pack those transition dates as a current open clock. Official (b)(1)(i)–(v) lookback / restatement-date / recovery-amount / impracticability / no-indemnification catalog exists, including official (b)(1)(i)(D) “An issuer's obligation to recover erroneously awarded compensation is not dependent on if or when the restated financial statements are filed”; name official (b)(1)(i)–(v), do not build that catalog, and do not flatten that sentence into already-taught 10-K / 10-Q clocks. Official (b)(1)(iv)(C), if named, is written “the requirements of26 U.S.C. 401(a)(13)” with **no space** after “of”; do not silently insert a space, and do not treat a LII U.S.C. link wrap as official typography that adds a space. Official (c) exemptions and official (d) definitions (including “Executive Officer,” “Financial reporting measures,” “Incentive-based compensation,” and “Received”) exist; name them, do not build those definition engines. Do not invent a numeric clock for “reasonably promptly.” Do not flatten 10D-1 into already-taught Form 8-K Item 4.02, packed 12b-20, packed 13a-14, or already-taught 10-K / 10-Q clocks.

**A foreign private issuer’s annual report on Form 20-F is due within four months after fiscal year-end, not on the 10-K 60 / 75 / 90 clocks; current reports on Form 6-K are furnished promptly after the material is made public in the official home-market / exchange / shareholder channels, not on an invented four-business-day 8-K clock (checked 2026-11-12).** The already-taught domestic 10-K / 10-Q / 8-K / 12b-25 clocks, the Form 10-S proposal sentence, and the FPI Form 4 sentence (directors and officers of foreign private issuers file Form 4 from 2026-03-18) stay as written. This section adds the official FPI periodic clocks so a cleaner does not apply 60 / 75 / 90 to a 20-F, or treat a 6-K as a domestic 8-K. Do not edit `examples/p2/09_filing_dates_toy.py` or `examples/p2/17_xbrl_pit_10b51_toy.py`. No filer-status planning advice. This item is quiz-only; there is no new lab script.

Official [Form 20-F](https://www.sec.gov/files/form20-f.pdf) General Instruction A.(a): any foreign private issuer, other than an asset-backed issuer, may use the form as a Section 12 registration statement or as an annual or transition report under Section 13(a) or 15(d). A.(b): “A foreign private issuer must file its annual report on this Form within the four months after the end of the fiscal year covered by the report.” Official [Form 6-K](https://www.sec.gov/files/form6-k.pdf) General Instruction B: an issuer furnishing a report on the form shall furnish information, not required on Form 40-F or previously furnished, that it (i) makes or is required to make public pursuant to the law of its domicile or organization, or (ii) files or is required to file with a stock exchange on which its securities are traded and which was made public by that exchange, or (iii) distributes or is required to distribute to its security holders, when that information is material as listed (including financial condition and results of operations, and a material cybersecurity incident). “This report is required to be furnished promptly after the material contained in the report is made public as described above. The information and documents furnished in this report shall not be deemed to be “filed” for the purposes of Section 18 of the Exchange Act or otherwise subject to the liabilities of that section.” [17 CFR 240.13a-16(b)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/17/240.13a-16): such reports “shall be transmitted promptly after the information required by Form 6-K is made public by the issuer, by the country of its domicile or under the laws of which it was incorporated or organized, or by a foreign securities exchange with which the issuer has filed the information.” 13a-16(c) repeats that furnished 6-K reports are not deemed filed for section 18.

Do not apply the 10-K 60 / 75 / 90 or 10-Q 40 / 45 clocks to a 20-F, or invent a four-business-day (or “promptly = 10 days”) clock for Form 6-K. Do not treat a 6-K as “filed” for Section 18 in the usual case, or as a substitute 10-Q. Form 40-F is a different MJDS form; do not conflate it with 20-F. 13a-16 does not apply to issuers already filing 10-K / 10-Q / 8-K, or to issuers of ADRs for securities of a foreign issuer (the depositary), as listed in 13a-16(a).

## 17 CFR 240.13f-1: Reporting by institutional investment managers of information with respect to accounts over which they exercise investment discretion (checked 2026-11-19)

Already-taught Form 13F 45-day awareness sentence, and the shipped FAQ-date sentence in section C (`3Q 2026 Form 13F due 2026-11-16 (SEC FAQ table: November 16, 2026)`), stay as written. Do not touch `examples/p2/09_filing_dates_toy.py` / `.out`. This box packs the whole current official 13f-1 section: official (a)(1) is triggered by aggregate fair market value of at least $100,000,000 on the last trading day of any month of any calendar year; the report on Form 13F is due within 45 days after the last day of such calendar year and within 45 days after the last day of each of the first three calendar quarters of the subsequent calendar year. Official typography uses typographic U+201C/U+201D around both “investment descretion” (official typo, first quoted term of official (b)) and “investment discretion” (correct spelling, later in official (b)); official (c) uses unquoted “investment descretion” and typographic quotes around “section 13(f) securities.” Do not tidy either spelling into the other. Official typography is that only securities of a class on the most recent Commission list shall be counted and reported. Official CITA is “[43 FR 26705, June 22, 1978, as amended at 44 FR 3034, Jan. 15, 1979; 64 FR 2849, Jan. 19, 1999; 76 FR 71876, Nov. 21, 2011].” Official SECAUTH typography is `(Secs. 3(b), 13(f) and 23 of the Exchange Act (15 U.S.C. 78c(b), 78m(f) and 78w))`. Official 249.325 is named only; do not pack it as a second core. This is not the already-taught 13F 45-day awareness sentence. Learning only; no filer or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.13f-1](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13f-1): Official (a)(1) “Every institutional investment manager which exercises investment discretion with respect to accounts holding section 13(f) securities, as defined in paragraph (c) of this section, having an aggregate fair market value on the last trading day of any month of any calendar year of at least $100,000,000 shall file a report on Form 13F (§ 249.325 of this chapter) with the Commission within 45 days after the last day of such calendar year and within 45 days after the last day of each of the first three calendar quarters of the subsequent calendar year.” Official (a)(2) “An amendment to a Form 13F (§ 249.325 of this chapter) report, other than one reporting only holdings that were not previously reported in a public filing for the same period, must set forth the complete text of the Form 13F. Amendments must be numbered sequentially.” Official (b) “For the purposes of this rule, “investment descretion” has the meaning set forth in section 3(a)(35) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78c(a)(35)). An institutional investment manager shall also be deemed to exercise “investment discretion” with respect to all accounts over which any person under its control exercises investment discretion.” Official (c) “For purposes of this rule “section 13(f) securities” shall mean equity securities of a class described in section 13(d)(1) of the Act that are admitted to trading on a national securities exchange or quoted on the automated quotation system of a registered securities association. In determining what classes of securities are section 13(f) securities, an institutional investment manager may rely on the most recent list of such securities published by the Commission pursuant to section 13(f)(4) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78m(f)(4)). Only securities of a class on such list shall be counted in determining whether an institutional investment manager must file a report under this rule (§ 240.13f-1(a)) and only those securities shall be reported in such report. Where a person controls the issuer of a class of equity securities which are “section 13(f) securities” as defined in this rule, those securities shall not be deemed to be “section 13(f) securities” with respect to the controlling person, provided that such person does not otherwise exercise investment descretion with respect to accounts with fair market value of at least $100,000,000 within the meaning of paragraph (a) of this section.” Official caption typography is “Reporting by institutional investment managers of information with respect to accounts over which they exercise investment discretion.” Official source line: “[43 FR 26705, June 22, 1978, as amended at 44 FR 3034, Jan. 15, 1979; 64 FR 2849, Jan. 19, 1999; 76 FR 71876, Nov. 21, 2011].” Official SECAUTH typography is `(Secs. 3(b), 13(f) and 23 of the Exchange Act (15 U.S.C. 78c(b), 78m(f) and 78w))`. Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17.

Official 249.325 is named only. Do not rewrite the already-taught 13F 45-day awareness sentence, and do not invent a new Form 13F due date.

## 17 CFR 240.13f-2: Reporting by institutional investment managers regarding gross short position and activity information (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 13f-1 stays as written. Do not rewrite the already-taught p3 `pairs-stat-arb` Form SHO awareness box, and do not invent that 2026 vendor Form SHO data is official or backtest-ready. This box packs the whole current official 13f-2 section: a report on Form SHO is due within 14 calendar days after the end of each calendar month. For section 12-registered or 15(d)-reporting equity, the official thresholds are a monthly average gross short position at the close of regular trading hours of $10 million or more, or 2.5 percent or more of shares outstanding. For equity not so registered or 15(d)-reporting, the official threshold is a gross short position of $500,000 or more at the close of regular trading hours on any settlement date during the calendar month. Official typography italicizes `institutional investment manager`, `equity security`, `investment discretion`, `gross short position`, and `regular trading hours`. Official typography uses typographic quotes around “EDGAR.” Official (b)(4) typography is “without inclusion of any offsetting economic positions such as shares of the equity security or derivatives of such equity security” (do not tidy that into “does not include”). Official (a)(3) typography is that the Commission will publish, on an aggregated basis, certain information. Official CITA is “[88 FR 75185, Nov. 1, 2023].” Official API XML has no SECAUTH; do not invent one. Official 13f-1(b), official 249.332, official 3a11-1, official 242.200(a), and official 242.600(b)(77) are named only. Learning only; no filer or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.13f-2](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.13f-2): Official (a) “An institutional investment manager shall file a report on Form SHO (referenced in 17 CFR 249.332), in accordance with the form's instructions, with the Commission within 14 calendar days after the end of each calendar month with regard to:” Official (a)(1) “Each equity security that is of a class of securities that is registered pursuant to section 12 of the Exchange Act or for which the issuer of that class of securities is required to file reports pursuant to section 15(d) of the Exchange Act over which the institutional investment manager and all accounts over which the institutional investment manager (or any person under the institutional investment manager's control) has investment discretion with respect to either:” Official (a)(1)(i) “A monthly average gross short position at the close of regular trading hours in the equity security with a U.S. dollar value of $10 million or more; or” Official (a)(1)(ii) “A monthly average gross short position at the close of regular trading hours as a percentage of shares outstanding in the equity security of 2.5 percent or more; and” Official (a)(2) “Each equity security that is of a class of securities that is not registered pursuant to section 12 of the Exchange Act or for which the issuer of that class of securities is not required to file reports pursuant to section 15(d) of the Exchange Act over which the institutional investment manager and all accounts over which the institutional investment manager (or any person under the institutional investment manager's control) has investment discretion with respect to a gross short position in the equity security with a U.S. dollar value of $500,000 or more at the close of regular trading hours on any settlement date during the calendar month.” Official (a)(3) “Form SHO and any amendments thereto must be filed with the Commission via the Commission's Electronic Data Gathering, Analysis, and Retrieval system (“EDGAR”), in accordance with 17 CFR part 232 (Regulation S-T). The Commission will publish, on an aggregated basis, certain information regarding each equity security reported by institutional investment managers on Form SHO and filed with the Commission via EDGAR.” Official (b) “For the purposes of this section:” Official (b)(1) “The term <em>institutional investment manager</em> has the same meaning as in section 13(f)(6)(A) of the Exchange Act.” Official (b)(2) “The term <em>equity security</em> has the same meaning as in section 3(a)(11) of the Exchange Act and § 240.3a11-1 (Rule 3a11-1).” Official (b)(3) “The term <em>investment discretion</em> has the same meaning as in § 240.13f-1(b) (Rule 13f-1(b)).” Official (b)(4) “The term <em>gross short position</em> means the number of shares of the equity security that are held short as a result of short sales as defined in 17 CFR 242.200(a) (Rule 200(a) of Regulation SHO), without inclusion of any offsetting economic positions such as shares of the equity security or derivatives of such equity security.” Official (b)(5) “The term <em>regular trading hours</em> has the same meaning as in 17 CFR 242.600(b)(77) (Rule 600(b)(77)).” Official caption typography is “Reporting by institutional investment managers regarding gross short position and activity information.” Official source line: “[88 FR 75185, Nov. 1, 2023].” Official API XML has no SECAUTH; do not invent one. Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17.

Official 13f-1(b) / 249.332 / 3a11-1 / 242.200(a) / 242.600(b)(77) are named only. Do not rewrite the already-taught p3 Form SHO awareness box, and do not invent that 2026 vendor Form SHO data is official or backtest-ready.

official caption typography is Reporting by institutional investment managers regarding gross short position and activity information.

13f-2(a)(1): Each equity security that is of a class of securities that is registered pursuant to section 12 of the Exchange Act or for which the issuer of that class of securities is required to file reports pursuant to section 15(d) of the Exchange Act over which the institutional investment manager and all accounts over which the institutional investment manager (or any person under the institutional investment manager's control) has investment discretion with respect to either:
13f-2(a)(1)(i): A monthly average gross short position at the close of regular trading hours in the equity security with a U.S. dollar value of $10 million or more; or
13f-2(a)(1)(ii): A monthly average gross short position at the close of regular trading hours as a percentage of shares outstanding in the equity security of 2.5 percent or more; and
13f-2(a)(2): Each equity security that is of a class of securities that is not registered pursuant to section 12 of the Exchange Act or for which the issuer of that class of securities is not required to file reports pursuant to section 15(d) of the Exchange Act over which the institutional investment manager and all accounts over which the institutional investment manager (or any person under the institutional investment manager's control) has investment discretion with respect to a gross short position in the equity security with a U.S. dollar value of $500,000 or more at the close of regular trading hours on any settlement date during the calendar month.

## 17 CFR 240.14Ad-1: Report of proxy voting record (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 13f-1 / 13f-2 stay as written. This box packs the whole current official 14Ad-1 section: an institutional investment manager required to file reports under section 13(f) must file an annual report on Form N-PX not later than August 31 of each year, for the most recent 12-month period ended June 30, with official first-year and exit-year exceptions, including official (c) “not later than March 1 of the immediately following calendar year.” Official typography italicizes <em>Voting power</em> in official (d)(1) and italicizes only <em>Exercise</em> in official (d)(2) “Exercise of voting power.” Official typography uses typographic U+201C/U+201D around the defined terms “initial filing” and “final filing” in the official “For purposes of this paragraph …” sentences. Official (b)/(c) exemption sentences also use unquoted initial filing / final filing; do not tidy the unquoted uses and the quoted defined terms into one another. Official CITA is “[87 FR 78808, Dec. 22, 2022]”. Official API XML has no SECAUTH; do not invent a source line. Packed 13f-1 and official Form N-PX / § 249.326 / § 274.129 / § 249.325 are named only; do not pack those form catalogs. Learning only; no issuer or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.14Ad-1](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.14Ad-1): Official (a) “Subject to paragraphs (b) and (c) of this section, every institutional investment manager (as that term is defined in section 13(f)(6)(A) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78m(f)(6)(A))) that is required to file reports under section 13(f) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78m(f)) must file an annual report on Form N-PX (§§ 249.326 and 274.129 of this chapter) not later than August 31 of each year, for the most recent 12-month period ended June 30, containing the institutional investment manager's proxy voting record for each shareholder vote pursuant to sections 14A(a) and (b) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78n-1(a) and (b)) with respect to each security over which the manager exercised voting power (as defined in paragraph (d) of this section).” Official (b) “An institutional investment manager is not required to file a report on Form N-PX (§§ 249.326 and 274.129 of this chapter) for the 12-month period ending June 30 of the calendar year in which the manager's initial filing on Form 13F (§ 249.325 of this chapter) is due pursuant to § 240.13f-1. For purposes of this paragraph (b), “initial filing” on Form 13F means any quarterly filing on Form 13F if no filing on Form 13F was required for the immediately preceding calendar quarter.” Official (c) “An institutional investment manager is not required to file a report on Form N-PX (§§ 249.326 and 274.129 of this chapter) with respect to any shareholder vote at a meeting that occurs after September 30 of the calendar year in which the manager's final filing on Form 13F (§ 249.325 of this chapter) is due pursuant to § 240.13f-1. An institutional investment manager is required to file a Form N-PX for the period July 1 through September 30 of the calendar year in which the manager's final filing on Form 13F is due pursuant to § 240.13f-1; this filing is required to be made not later than March 1 of the immediately following calendar year. For purposes of this paragraph (c), “final filing” on Form 13F means any quarterly filing on Form 13F if no filing on Form 13F is required for the immediately subsequent calendar quarter.” Official (d) “For purposes of this section:” Official (d)(1) “<em>Voting power</em> means the ability, through any contract, arrangement, understanding, or relationship, to vote a security or direct the voting of a security, including the ability to determine whether to vote a security or to recall a loaned security.” Official (d)(2) “<em>Exercise</em> of voting power means using voting power to influence a voting decision with respect to a security.” Official caption typography is “Report of proxy voting record.” Official source line: “[87 FR 78808, Dec. 22, 2022]”. Official API XML has no SECAUTH; do not invent a source line. Official typography italicizes <em>Voting power</em> in official (d)(1) and italicizes only <em>Exercise</em> in official (d)(2) “Exercise of voting power.” Official typography uses typographic U+201C/U+201D around the defined terms “initial filing” and “final filing” in the official “For purposes of this paragraph …” sentences; official (b)/(c) exemption sentences also use unquoted initial filing / final filing — do not tidy the unquoted uses and the quoted defined terms into one another. Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17.

## 17 CFR 244.100(a): Regulation G non-GAAP financial measure presentation and reconciliation (checked 2026-11-12)

Packed 10D-1 above stays as written. Do not rewrite that already-shipped box. This section packs official 244.100(a) only, so a student does not tidy official (b)’s “in light of the circumstances under which it is presented, not misleading” into packed 10b-5 / 12b-20 “in the light of” or into “they are presented,” tidy “by Web cast,” “the registrant's Web site,” “the location of the web site,” or “web sites” into “website,” “webcast,” packed 4370 / 2264 “Web site,” or packed 3150 “website,” silently renumber official Note 1’s “paragraphs (a)(1)(i) and (a)(1)(ii),” or flatten this rule into already-taught earnings / 10-K / 10-Q clocks, packed 13a-14, packed 12b-20, or packed 10D-1. Title 17 is up to date as of 2026-11-09. Learning only; no issuer, disclosure, or investment advice.

[17 CFR 244.100](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-244.100) official caption typography is “General rules regarding disclosure of non-GAAP financial measures.” Official (a): “Whenever a registrant, or person acting on its behalf, publicly discloses material information that includes a non-GAAP financial measure, the registrant must accompany that non-GAAP financial measure with: (1) A presentation of the most directly comparable financial measure calculated and presented in accordance with Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (GAAP); and (2) A reconciliation (by schedule or other clearly understandable method), which shall be quantitative for historical non-GAAP measures presented, and quantitative, to the extent available without unreasonable efforts, for forward-looking information, of the differences between the non-GAAP financial measure disclosed or released with the most comparable financial measure or measures calculated and presented in accordance with GAAP identified in paragraph (a)(1) of this section.” Official (a)(1) typography is “Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (GAAP)” with initial capitals. Official (c)(2) later uses lowercase “generally accepted accounting principles in the United States”; do not tidy one into the other.

Name official (b). Official (b) typography is “in light of the circumstances under which it is presented, not misleading” (not packed 10b-5 / 12b-20 “in the light of,” not 12b-20’s “they are made,” and not “they are presented”). Name official (c) and official (d). Official (d) typography is “the entity resulting therefrom or an entity that is a party thereto,” with no Oxford comma. Official Note 1 typography is “by Web cast” (two words, capital W) and cites “paragraphs (a)(1)(i) and (a)(1)(ii)” even though official (a) is numbered (a)(1)/(a)(2); do not silently renumber the note. Official Note 1(i) typography is “the registrant's Web site” (capital W, two words); official Note 1(ii) typography is “the location of the web site” (lowercase w, two words); official Note 2(iii) typography is “web sites” (lowercase). Do not tidy these into “website” / “webcast,” packed 4370 / 2264 “Web site,” or packed 3150 “website.” Name 17 CFR 244.101 / 244.102 and Regulation S-K Item 10(e) / 17 CFR 229.10 only; do not pack them as second cores, and do not flatten 244.102 into packed 10b-5. Official 244.102 typography, if named, includes “compliance or non-compliance,” and “shall in itself affect.” Do not flatten 244.100 into already-taught earnings / 10-K / 10-Q clocks, packed 13a-14, packed 12b-20, or packed 10D-1.

## 17 CFR 240.14c-6: False or misleading statements (checked 2026-11-12)

Packed 14a-9 / 14c-3(a) / 12b-20 stay as written. This section packs official 14c-6(a) + official (b) (the whole current section). This is the information-statement companion to packed 14a-9. It is not packed 14a-9, not packed 14c-3(a), and not packed 12b-20. Official 14c-6 has no official (c) and no Note; packed 14a-9’s official (c) / Note catalog stays named only under 14a-9. Do not paste packed 14a-9(a) or 14a-9(b) over this rule. Do not flatten this rule into packed 14a-9, packed 14c-3(a), packed 12b-20, packed 10b-5, already-taught 14a-8, or Form 8-K Item 5.07. Learning only; no issuer, information-statement, or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.14c-6](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.14c-6) packs official (a) + official (b). Official caption typography is “False or misleading statements.”

Official (a): “No information statement shall contain any statement which, at the time and in the light of the circumstances under which it is made, is false or misleading with respect to any material fact, or which omits to state any material fact necessary in order to make the statements therein not false or misleading or necessary to correct any statement in any earlier communication with respect to the same meeting or subject matter which has become false or misleading.” Official typography is “in the light of the circumstances under which it is made” (same singular “it is made” as packed 14a-9(a); do not tidy that into packed 10b-5’s “under which they were made” or packed 12b-20’s “under which they are made not misleading”). Official typography is “any earlier communication with respect to the same meeting or subject matter” — **not** packed 14a-9(a)’s “with respect to the solicitation of a proxy for the same meeting or subject matter.” Official (a) is limited to “No information statement shall contain” and does not use packed 14a-9’s “proxy statement, form of proxy, notice of meeting or other communication, written or oral.”

Official (b): “The fact that an information statement has been filed with or examined by the Commission shall not be deemed a finding by the Commission that such material is accurate or complete or not false or misleading, or that the Commission has passed upon the merits of or approved any statement contained therein or any matter to be acted upon by security holders. No representation contrary to the foregoing shall be made.” Official typography is “an information statement has been filed with or examined by the Commission” — **not** packed 14a-9(b)’s “a proxy statement, form of proxy or other soliciting material.” Official typography is two-word “security holders.” Official source line: “[31 FR 262, Jan. 8, 1966].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-09.

Packed 14a-9 (including official (b), official (c), and the Note, which remain named only under that already-printed box — do not pack 14a-9(b) as a new core), packed 14c-3(a), packed 12b-20, packed 10b-5, leftover 14c-2 / 14c-5, and 17 CFR 240.14c-4 exist and are named only.

## 17 CFR 240.15c1-9: Use of pro forma balance sheets (checked 2026-11-12)

Official typography italicizes the defined term, including the trailing comma, and does **not** put quotation marks around it. Learning only; no issuer, broker, or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.15c1-9](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.15c1-9): “The term <em>manipulative, deceptive, or other fraudulent device or contrivance,</em> as used in section 15(c)(1) of the Act, is hereby defined to include the use of financial statements purporting to give effect to the receipt and application of any part of the proceeds from the sale or exchange of securities, unless the assumptions upon which each such financial statement is based are clearly set forth as part of the caption to each such statement in type at least as large as that used generally in the body of the statement.” Official caption typography is “Use of pro forma balance sheets.” Official typography is two-word “pro forma” (no hyphen). Official typography italicizes the defined term, including the trailing comma, and does **not** put quotation marks around it. Official typography is “as part of the caption to each such statement in type at least as large as that used generally in the body of the statement” (no numeric point size). Official authority typography is `15 U.S.C. 78<em>o</em>` (the official italic letter-O / “78o” form). Do not invent a space in that cite, and do not treat a LII U.S.C. link wrap as official typography that adds a space. Official source line: “[13 FR 8205, Dec. 22, 1948].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-09.

## 17 CFR 240.12b-15: Amendments (checked 2026-11-12)

The already-printed XBRL 10-K / 10-K/A example stays as written; this box is the official amendment typography, not a second core of the XBRL box. Do not invent that “10-K/A” is the only amendment form (official typography is e.g., “10-K/A,” with the comma inside the closing quote). Official 13a-14(a) amendments “must include new certifications,” official 13a-14(b) amendments “must be accompanied by new certifications,” and official 13a-14(d) amendments “must include a new certification” — do not tidy those three official phrasings into one another. Do not flatten this rule into packed 13a-14, already-taught Form 10-K / 10-Q clocks, the already-printed XBRL `filed`-date box, packed 12b-20, packed 14c-6, or packed 244.100. Do not rewrite the already-printed XBRL box, and do not build a Regulation S-T engine. Learning only; no issuer, filing-agent, or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.12b-15](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-15): “All amendments must be filed under cover of the form amended, marked with the letter “A” to designate the document as an amendment, e.g., “10-K/A,” and in compliance with pertinent requirements applicable to statements and reports. Amendments filed pursuant to this section must set forth the complete text of each item as amended. Amendments must be numbered sequentially and be filed separately for each statement or report amended. Amendments to a statement may be filed either before or after registration becomes effective. Amendments must be signed on behalf of the registrant by a duly authorized representative of the registrant. An amendment to any report required to include the certifications as specified in § 240.13a-14(a) or § 240.15d-14(a) must include new certifications by each principal executive and principal financial officer of the registrant, and an amendment to any report required to be accompanied by the certifications as specified in § 240.13a-14(b) or § 240.15d-14(b) must be accompanied by new certifications by each principal executive and principal financial officer of the registrant. An amendment to any report required to include the certifications as specified in § 240.13a-14(d) or § 240.15d-14(d) must include a new certification by an individual specified in § 240.13a-14(e) or § 240.15d-14(e), as applicable. The requirements of the form being amended will govern the number of copies to be filed in connection with a paper format amendment. Electronic filers satisfy the provisions dictating the number of copies by filing one copy of the amendment in electronic format. See § 232.309 of this chapter (Rule 309 of Regulation S-T).” Official caption typography is “Amendments.” Official typography uses typographic quotes (U+201C / U+201D) around “A” and around “10-K/A,” with the comma **inside** the closing quote. Official typography is “e.g.,” (comma after e.g.). Official typography is “complete text of each item as amended” and “numbered sequentially and be filed separately for each statement or report amended.” Official source line: “[68 FR 36665, June 18, 2003, as amended at 70 FR 1620, Jan. 7, 2005].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-09.

Already-printed XBRL `filed`-date box (including the Apple 10-K / 10-K/A example), packed 13a-14 / named 15d-14, already-taught Form 10-K / 10-Q clocks, and 17 CFR 232.309 / Regulation S-T Rule 309 (the electronic one-copy sentence already appears in the packed slice; do not build a Regulation S-T engine) exist and are named only.

## 17 CFR 240.12b-6: When securities are deemed to be registered (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 12b-15, already-taught Form 10-K / 10-Q clocks, leftover 12g-1 / 12g-4, packed Section 16(b), and already-taught Form 4 clocks stay as written. This box is a different filing-date trap: a class is not deemed registered for sections 13, 14, 15(d) and 16, and is not subject to sections 13, 14 and 16, until the section 12 registration statement has become **effective**. Do not start those clocks at the filing date. Do not invent 15(d) into the official second list. Do not flatten this rule into those already-shipped boxes. Learning only; no issuer, filing-agent, or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.12b-6](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-6): “A class of securities with respect to which a registration statement has been filed pursuant to section 12 of the act shall be deemed to be registered for the purposes of sections 13, 14, 15(d) and 16 of the act and the rules and regulations thereunder only when such statement has become effective as provided in section 12, and securities of said class shall not be subject to sections 13, 14 and 16 of the act until such statement has become effective as provided in section 12.” Official caption typography is “When securities are deemed to be registered.” Official typography is “section 12 of the act” (lowercase act). Official typography is “sections 13, 14, 15(d) and 16 of the act” (no Oxford comma before “and 16”). Official typography in the second clause is “sections 13, 14 and 16 of the act” (no 15(d); no Oxford comma). Official authority typography is 15 U.S.C. 78c, 78n, 78p, 78*l* (italic letter-L). Official source line: “[30 FR 482, Jan. 14, 1965].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17.

Packed 12b-15, already-taught Form 10-K / 10-Q clocks, leftover 12g-1 / 12g-4, packed Section 16(b), and already-taught Form 4 clocks exist and are named only.

## 17 CFR 240.12d2-1: Suspension of trading (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 12b-6 and the already-taught delisting-returns box stay as written. This box is a different rule: official (a) “A national securities exchange may suspend from trading a security listed and registered thereon in accordance with its rules. Such exchange shall promptly notify the Commission of any such suspension, the effective date thereof, and the reasons therefor.” Do not invent a numeric clock for “promptly.” Official (b) “Any such suspension may be continued until such time as it shall appear to the Commission that such suspension is designed to evade the provisions of section 12(d) and the rules and regulations thereunder relating to the withdrawal and striking of a security from listing and registration. During the continuance of such suspension the exchange shall notify the Commission promptly of any change in the reasons for the suspension. Upon the restoration to trading of any security suspended under this rule, the exchange shall notify the Commission promptly of the effective date thereof.” Official (c) “Suspension of trading shall not terminate the registration of any security.” Official typography is “the reasons therefor,” “designed to evade the provisions of section 12(d),” and “withdrawal and striking.” Official SECAUTH typography is 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>, with official italic on the letter l. ## 17 CFR 240.24b-3: Information filed by issuers and others under sections 12, 13, 14, and 16 (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 12d2-1 stays as written. This box is a different rule and packs the whole current official 24b-3 section (official (a)–(b)): except as otherwise provided in this section and in § 240.17a-6, each exchange shall keep available to the public under reasonable regulations as to the manner of inspection, during reasonable office hours, all information regarding a security registered on such exchange which is filed with it pursuant to section 12, 13, 14, or 16, or any rules or regulations thereunder. This requirement shall not apply to any information to the disclosure of which objection has been filed pursuant to § 240.24b-2, which objection shall not have been overruled by the Commission pursuant to section 24(b). The making of such information available pursuant to this section shall not be deemed a representation by any exchange as to the accuracy, completeness, or genuineness thereof. Official (b): in the case of an application for registration of a security pursuant to section 12 an exchange may delay making available the information contained therein until it has certified to the Commission its approval of such security for listing and registration. Official caption typography is “Information filed by issuers and others under sections 12, 13, 14, and 16.” Official (a) typography is “pursuant to section 12, 13, 14, or 16” (body uses “or,” not the caption’s “and”); do not tidy “and” / “or.” Official typography is “under reasonable regulations as to the manner of inspection, during reasonable office hours.” Official typography is “shall not be deemed a representation by any exchange as to the accuracy, completeness, or genuineness thereof.” Official typography is “pursuant to § 240.24b-2” and “pursuant to section 24(b)” — do not tidy in “of the Act” from official 24b-1. Official (b) typography is “pursuant to section 12 an exchange may delay” (no comma after “section 12”). Official SECAUTH typography is (Sec. 24, 48 Stat. 901, as amended; 15 U.S.C. 78x) (official API XML has this SECAUTH; do not invent italics). Official CITA is “[16 FR 3109, Apr. 10, 1951].” Name official 24b-2, official 17a-6, and official 24b-1 only; do not pack those companions. Do not tidy official 24b-1 typography into this box: official 24b-1 uses “section 24(b) of the Act,” official SECAUTH is (Sec. 24, 48 Stat. 901; 15 U.S.C. 78x) (no “as amended”), and official CITA is “[13 FR 8214, Dec. 22, 1948].” Learning only; no issuer, filer, or investment advice.

## 17 CFR 240.12d1-1: Registration effective as to class or series (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 12d2-1 and packed 12b-6 stay as written. This box is a different rule: official (a) “An application filed pursuant to section 12 (b) and (c) of the act for registration of a security on a national securities exchange shall be deemed to apply for registration of the entire class of such security. Registration shall become effective, as provided in section 12(d) of the act, (1) as to the shares or amounts of such class then issued, and (2), without further application for registration, upon issuance as to additional shares or amounts of such class then or thereafter authorized.” Official typography is “section 12 (b) and (c) of the act” (spaces around the lettered paragraphs; official (a) uses lowercase “act”). Official (b) “This section shall apply to classes of securities of which a specified number of shares or amounts was registered or registered upon notice of issuance, and to applications for registration filed, prior to the close of business on January 28, 1954, as well as to classes registered, or applications filed, thereafter.” Official (c) “This section shall not affect the right of a national securities exchange to require the issuer of a registered security to file documents with or pay fees to the exchange in connection with the modification of such security or the issuance of additional shares or amounts.” Official (d) “If a class of security is issuable in two or more series with different terms, each such series shall be deemed a separate class for the purposes of this section.” Official caption typography is “Registration effective as to class or series.” Official SECAUTH typography is 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em> with official italic on the letter l. Official API XML has no CITA; do not invent a source line. Do not flatten this box into packed 12d2-1 or packed 12b-6. Learning only; no issuer, filer, or investment advice.

## 17 CFR 240.12d1-2: Effectiveness of registration (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 12d2-1 and packed 12b-6 stay as written. This box is a different rule: the Form 8-A effectiveness clocks under 12(b) and 12(g) are not the same. Official (a) “A request for acceleration of the effective date of registration pursuant to section 12(d) of the act and § 240.12d1-1 shall be made in writing by either the registrant, the exchange, or both and shall briefly describe the reasons therefor.” Official typography is “the reasons therefor.” Official (b) “A registration statement on Form 8-A (17 CFR 249.208a) for the registration of a class of securities under Section 12(b) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>(b)) shall become effective:” Official (b)(1) “If a class of securities is not concurrently being registered under the Securities Act of 1933 (“Securities Act”), upon the later of receipt by the Commission of certification from the national securities exchange or the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission; or” Official (b)(2) “If a class of securities is concurrently being registered under the Securities Act, upon the later of the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission, receipt by the Commission of certification from the national securities exchange listed on the Form 8-A or effectiveness of the Securities Act registration statement relating to the class of securities.” Official (c) “A registration statement on Form 8-A (17 CFR 249.208a) for the registration of a class of securities under Section 12(g) of the Act (15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>(g)) shall become effective:” Official (c)(1) “If a class of securities is not concurrently being registered under the Securities Act, upon the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission; or” Official (c)(2) “If class of securities is concurrently being registered under the Securities Act, upon the later of the filing of the Form 8-A with the Commission or the effectiveness of the Securities Act registration statement relating to the class of securities.” Official (c)(2) typography is “If class of securities is concurrently being registered under the Securities Act” — no “a” after “If”; official (b)(1), (b)(2), and (c)(1) do use “If a class of securities”. Do not tidy (c)(2) into “If a class.” Official 12(b) clocks are the later of Form 8-A filing and exchange certification (and, if a Securities Act registration is concurrent, the effectiveness of that registration statement as well). Official 12(g) clocks do not use exchange certification. Official typography uses typographic quotes around “Securities Act”. Official (b)/(c) typography is “Section 12(b) of the Act” / “Section 12(g) of the Act” (capital S and Act) and Form 8-A (“17 CFR 249.208a”). Official SECAUTH typography is 15 U.S.C. 78<em>l</em>, including 78<em>l</em>(b) and 78<em>l</em>(g). Official source line: “[19 FR 670, Feb. 5, 1954, as amended at 59 FR 55347, Nov. 7, 1994; 62 FR 39766, July 24, 1997].” Official 12d1-3 through 12d1-6 and official 12d2-2 are named only; do not pack those certification or removal catalogs. Do not flatten this box into packed 12d2-1 or packed 12b-6. Learning only; no issuer, filer, or investment advice.

## 17 CFR 232.12: Business hours of the Commission (checked 2026-11-19)

Already-taught Regulation S-T Rule 13 / 17 CFR 232.13 (the 5:30 p.m. Eastern filing-date cutoff) stays as written. This box is a different clock: official 232.12(c) says electronic filings may be submitted from **6 a.m. to 10 p.m.** Eastern, except Saturdays, Sundays, and Federal holidays. Do not invent that the electronic transmission window ends at 5:30 p.m. Do not invent that 232.12 deems every filing submitted by 10 p.m. filed on that same business day — that same-day 10 p.m. deeming is official 232.13(a)(3) / (a)(4) for specified forms, not 232.12. Do not tidy official “Eastern Daylight Saving Time” into “Savings,” and do not tidy the official (a) / (b) / (c) holiday typography into one another. Do not rewrite the already-taught Rule 13 box. Learning only; no filer, filing-agent, or investment advice.

[17 CFR 232.12](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-232.12): official (a) “The principal office of the Commission, at 100 F Street, NE., Washington, DC 20549, is open each day, except Saturdays, Sundays, and federal holidays, from 9 a.m. to 5:30 p.m., Eastern Standard Time or Eastern Daylight Saving Time, whichever is currently in effect, provided that hours for the filing of documents pursuant to the Acts or the rules and regulations thereunder are as set forth in paragraphs (b) and (c) of this section.” Official (b) “Filers may submit paper documents filed with or otherwise furnished to the Commission each day, except Saturdays, Sundays and federal holidays, from 8 a.m. to 5:30 p.m., Eastern Standard Time or Eastern Daylight Saving Time, whichever is currently in effect.” Official (c) “Electronic filings and other documents may be submitted to the Commission each day, except Saturdays, Sundays, and Federal holidays, from 6 a.m. to 10 p.m., Eastern Standard Time or Eastern Daylight Saving Time, whichever is currently in effect.” Official caption typography is “Business hours of the Commission.” Official typography italicizes “General.” / “Submissions made in paper.” / “Submissions by direct transmission.” / “provided that”, including the trailing period on those three lead-ins. ## 17 CFR 232.301: EDGAR Filer Manual (checked 2026-11-19)

Packed 232.12, packed 232.13, and packed 232.14 stay as written. This box is a different rule: “Filers must prepare electronic filings in the manner prescribed by the EDGAR Filer Manual, promulgated by the Commission, which sets forth the technical formatting requirements for electronic submissions. The requirements for becoming an EDGAR Filer and updating company data are set forth in the EDGAR Filer Manual, Volume I: “General Information,” Version 43 (March 2026). The requirements for filing on EDGAR are set forth in the updated EDGAR Filer Manual, Volume II: “EDGAR Filing,” Version 78 (September 2026). All EDGAR material referenced in this paragraph is incorporated by reference into this section with the approval of the Director of the Federal Register under 5 U.S.C. 552(a) and 1 CFR part 51. You must comply with these requirements in order for documents to be timely received and accepted. The EDGAR Filer Manual is made available for inspection by the Commission and the National Archives and Records Administration (NARA). The EDGAR Filer Manual may be obtained from the SEC's website at <em>https://www.sec.gov/edgar/filerinformation/current-edgar-filer-manual.</em> For information on the availability of the EDGAR Filer Manual at NARA, visit <em>www.archives.gov/federal-register/cfr/ibr-locations</em> or email <em>fr.inspection@nara.gov.</em>” Official typography uses typographic quotes around “General Information” and “EDGAR Filing.” Official typography italicizes those SEC and NARA addresses, including the period inside the italics after the SEC URL and after <em>fr.inspection@nara.gov.</em> Official typography is “You must comply with these requirements in order for documents to be timely received and accepted.” Official caption typography is “EDGAR Filer Manual.” Official source line: “[91 FR 63478, Oct. 6, 2026].” Do not pack the Filer Manual itself. ## 17 CFR 240.12b-23: Incorporation by reference (checked 2026-11-19)

The already-printed XBRL `filed`-date box and packed 12b-20 / 12b-15 / 12b-6 / 232.12 stay as written. This box is a different rule: official (a)–(e) say when information may be incorporated by reference. Official (a) allows incorporation “except as provided by this section or in the appropriate form.” Official (b) typography is “U.S. Generally Accepted Accounting Principles or International Financial Reporting Standards as issued by the International Accounting Standards Board, whichever is applicable” — do not tidy that to a bare “GAAP / IFRS.” Official (d) typography is second-person “You must include an active hyperlink,” with typographic quotes around “EDGAR”: Retrieval System (“EDGAR”). Official (e) forbids chain incorporation “unless expressly permitted or required.” Official (a)–(e) italic lead-ins include the trailing periods. Do not flatten this rule into the already-printed XBRL box or packed 12b-20 / 12b-15 / 12b-6 / 232.12, and do not rewrite the XBRL box. Learning only; no issuer, filer, or investment advice.

[17 CFR 240.12b-23](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-17/section-240.12b-23): official (a) “<em>Registration statement or report.</em> Except as provided by this section or in the appropriate form, information may be incorporated by reference in answer, or partial answer, to any item of a registration statement or report.” Official (b) “<em>Financial information.</em> Except as provided in the Commission's rules, financial information required to be given in comparative form for two or more fiscal years or periods must not be incorporated by reference unless the information incorporated by reference includes the entire period for which the comparative data is given. In the financial statements, incorporating by reference, or cross-referencing to, information outside of the financial statements is not permitted unless otherwise specifically permitted or required by the Commission's rules or by U.S. Generally Accepted Accounting Principles or International Financial Reporting Standards as issued by the International Accounting Standards Board, whichever is applicable.” Official (c) “<em>Exhibits.</em> Any document or part thereof filed with the Commission pursuant to any Act administered by the Commission may be incorporated by reference as an exhibit to any statement or report filed with the Commission by the same or any other person. Any document or part thereof filed with an exchange pursuant to the Act may be incorporated by reference as an exhibit to any statement or report filed with the exchange by the same or any other person. If any modification has occurred in the text of any document incorporated by reference since the filing thereof, the registrant must file with the reference a statement containing the text of any such modification and the date thereof.” Official (d) “<em>Hyperlinks.</em> You must include an active hyperlink to information incorporated into a registration statement or report by reference if such information is publicly available on the Commission's Electronic Data Gathering, Analysis and Retrieval System (“EDGAR”) at the time the registration statement or form is filed. For hyperlinking to exhibits, please refer to Item 601 of Regulation S-K (§ 229.601 of this chapter) or the appropriate form.” Official (e) “<em>General.</em> Include an express statement clearly describing the specific location of the information you are incorporating by reference. The statement must identify the document where the information was originally filed or submitted and the location of the information within that document. The statement must be made at the particular place where the information is required, if applicable. Information must not be incorporated by reference in any case where such incorporation would render the disclosure incomplete, unclear, or confusing. For example, unless expressly permitted or required, disclosure must not be incorporated by reference from a second document if that second document incorporates information pertinent to such disclosure by reference to a third document.” Official caption typography is “Incorporation by reference.” Official source line: “[84 FR 12727, Apr. 2, 2019].” Title 17 `up_to_date_as_of` 2026-11-17.

The already-printed XBRL `filed`-date box and packed 12b-20 / 12b-15 / 12b-6 / 232.12 exist and are named only; this box does not rewrite the XBRL box.

official caption typography is Incorporation by reference.
12b-23(a): Registration statement or report. Except as provided by this section or in the appropriate form, information may be incorporated by reference in answer, or partial answer, to any item of a registration statement or report.
12b-23(b): Financial information. Except as provided in the Commission's rules, financial information required to be given in comparative form for two or more fiscal years or periods must not be incorporated by reference unless the information incorporated by reference includes the entire period for which the comparative data is given. In the financial statements, incorporating by reference, or cross-referencing to, information outside of the financial statements is not permitted unless otherwise specifically permitted or required by the Commission's rules or by U.S. Generally Accepted Accounting Principles or International Financial Reporting Standards as issued by the International Accounting Standards Board, whichever is applicable.

Any document or part thereof filed with an exchange pursuant to the Act may be incorporated by reference as an exhibit to any statement or report filed with the exchange by the same or any other person.
